# MAgent improvements

**3539 verifiable improvements** across 24 categories, every one of them traced to a file and symbol that exists in this repository.

This file is generated by `scripts/build_improvements.py` and checked by `scripts/verify_improvements.py`. Re-run `make verify` after any change: an entry whose reference no longer resolves fails the build.

## How to read an entry

* `ref` is `path#symbol` for code, a tool, a CLI command or a test;
* a bare `ref` is a file that must exist (docs, workflows, packaging);
* `path#token` for Makefile targets and Markdown sections must appear verbatim.
